UNADILLA RIVER AT ROCKDALE NY, USGS site 01502500 (USGS-01502500), stream in Chenango County, New York, draining 520 square miles. Daily means from 1929-11-22 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
